013120:     function withTimeout(promise, timeoutMs, label) {
013121:         return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
013122:             const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
013123:                 reject(
013124:                     new Error(
013125:                         `${label || 'Operation'} timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`
013126:                     )
013127:                 );
013128:             }, timeoutMs);
013129: 
013130:             Promise.resolve(promise).then(
013131:                 value => {
013132:                     clearTimeout(timeoutId);
013133:                     resolve(value);
013134:                 },
013135:                 error => {
013136:                     clearTimeout(timeoutId);
013137:                     reject(error);
013138:                 }
013139:             );
013140:         });
013141:     }

025938:         personnelText
025939:     ) {
025940:         const cleanedName =
025941:             cleanRequirementName(requirementName);
025942: 
025943:         // Only the mission-definition "Required Personnel" row is actionable.
025944:         // "Required Personnel Available" belongs to Reward and Precondition and
025945:         // must never create dispatch demand.
025946:         if (!/^Personnel(?:\s+Requirements?)?$/i.test(cleanedName)) {
025947:             return [];
025948:         }
025949: 
025950:         const input = String(personnelText || '')
025951:             .replace(/[×✕]/g, 'x')
025952:             .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
025953:             .trim();
025954: 
025955:         if (!input) return [];
025956: 
025957:         const definitions = [
025958:             {
025959:                 name: 'Search Technician',
025960:                 patterns: [
025961:                     /(\d+)\s*(?:x\s*)?Search\s+Technician(?:s)?/gi,
025962:                     /Search\s+Technician(?:s)?\s*(?:x\s*)?(\d+)/gi
025963:                 ]
025964:             },
025965:             {
025966:                 name: 'SAR Commander',
025967:                 patterns: [
025968:                     /(\d+)\s*(?:x\s*)?SAR\s+Commander(?:s)?/gi,
025969:                     /SAR\s+Commander(?:s)?\s*(?:x\s*)?(\d+)/gi
025970:                 ]
025971:             }
025972:         ];
025973: 
025974:         const conversions = [];
025975: 
025976:         definitions.forEach(definition => {
025977:             let maximumRequired = 0;
025978: 
025979:             definition.patterns.forEach(pattern => {
025980:                 pattern.lastIndex = 0;
025981:                 let match;
025982: 
025983:                 while ((match = pattern.exec(input))) {
025984:                     const patternIsPrefix =
025985:                         pattern.source.startsWith('(\\d+)');
025986:                     const trailingText = input.slice(pattern.lastIndex);
025987: 
025988:                     if (
025989:                         !patternIsPrefix &&
025990:                         /^\s*x(?=\s|[A-Za-z])/i.test(trailingText)
025991:                     ) {
025992:                         continue;
025993:                     }
025994: 
025995:                     maximumRequired = Math.max(
025996:                         maximumRequired,
025997:                         Math.max(0, parseInt(match[1], 10) || 0)
025998:                     );
025999:                 }
026000:             });
026001: 
026002:             if (maximumRequired <= 0) return;
026003: 
026004:             const conversion =
026005:                 getSarPersonnelVehicleRequirement(
026006:                     definition.name,
026007:                     maximumRequired
026008:                 );
026009: 
026010:             if (conversion) conversions.push(conversion);
026011:         });

026218:                 'tbody tr, tr'
026219:             ).forEach(row => {
026220:                 const cells = Array.from(
026221:                     row.querySelectorAll('td')
026222:                 ).map(cell => normalise(
026223:                     cell.textContent
026224:                 ));
026225: 
026226:                 if (cells.length < 2) return;

026229:                     /^(?:Required\s+|Maximum amount of cars to tow|Cars to tow)/i

026232:                 ) {
026233:                     actionableRows += 1;
026234:                 }
026235:             });
026236: 
026237:             if (actionableRows > 0) {
026238:                 score += 100 +
026239:                     actionableRows;

026241: 
026242:             if (score > bestScore) {
026243:                 bestScore = score;
026244:                 bestTable = table;
026245:             }
026246:         });

026248:         return bestTable;

026762: 
026763:     function extractTowCarRequirementRows(doc) {
026764:         const rows = [];
026765:         let maximumCarsToTow = 0;
026766: 
026767:         doc.querySelectorAll('table.table, table').forEach(table => {
026768:             table.querySelectorAll('tbody tr, tr').forEach(tr => {
026769:                 const cells = Array.from(tr.querySelectorAll('td')).map(td => td.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim());
026770:                 if (cells.length < 2) return;
026771: 
026772:                 const label = cleanRequirementName(cells[0]);
026773:                 const valueText = cells[1].replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
026774: 
026775:                 if (!/^Maximum amount of cars to tow$/i.test(label)) return;
026776: 
026777:                 const cars = parseInt(valueText, 10);
026778:                 if (Number.isFinite(cars) && cars > maximumCarsToTow) {
026779:                     maximumCarsToTow = cars;
026780:                 }
026781:             });
026782:         });
026783:
